2026-10-24T04:36:09.6766898Z usr/libexec/anaconda/webui-desktop
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6767405Z #!/usr/bin/bash
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6767737Z
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6768227Z # This file is part of Cockpit.

2026-10-24T04:36:09.6777442Z # along with Cockpit; If not, see <http://www.gnu.org/licenses/>.
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6777918Z
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6778315Z # Run a local bridge, web server, and browser for a particular Cockpit page.
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6779094Z # This is useful for integration into .desktop files, for systems which don't
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6780076Z # have cockpit.socket enabled. The web server and browser run in an unshared
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6780843Z # network namespace, and thus are totally isolated from everything else.
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6781436Z #
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6781794Z # Usage:
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6782334Z #     cockpit-desktop /cockpit/@localhost/system/index.html -t default -r 0
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6783341Z # For testing purposes one can also make cockpit-ws bind to all addresses
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6784126Z # (this is insecure and should not be used in prodcuction):
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6784868Z #     cockpit-desktop /cockpit/@localhost/system/index.html -t default -r 1
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6785500Z #
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6785862Z set -eu
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6786142Z
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6786395Z THEME_ID="default"
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6786794Z WEBUI_REMOTE=0
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6787194Z while getopts t:r: option
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6787616Z do
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6787976Z     case "${option}"
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6788376Z         in
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6788768Z         t)THEME_ID=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6789223Z         r)WEBUI_REMOTE=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6789912Z         *) echo "Usage: $0 [-t THEME_ID] [-r WEBUI_REMOTE]" >&2
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6790455Z        exit 1 ;;
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6790842Z     esac
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6791235Z done
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6791707Z
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6792052Z URL_PATH="/cockpit/@localhost/anaconda-webui/index.html"
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6792516Z
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6792769Z WEBUI_ADDRESS="127.0.0.1"
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6793205Z if [[ "$WEBUI_REMOTE" == "1" ]]
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6793650Z then
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6794020Z     WEBUI_ADDRESS="0.0.0.0"
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6794445Z fi
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6794715Z
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6795076Z echo "WEBUI_ADDRESS=$WEBUI_ADDRESS" > /tmp/webui-cockpit-ws.env
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6795692Z systemctl start webui-cockpit-ws

2026-10-24T04:36:09.6796360Z # Function to setup user-related environment
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6796881Z setup_user_environment() {
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6797435Z     # PKEXEC_UID is the uid for "gnome-initial-setup" or "liveuser"
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6798073Z     # depending on how the installer gets started.
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6798591Z     #
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6799128Z     # It's unset on non-live-images, so we just use the current user then (root)
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6799987Z     if [ -n "${PKEXEC_UID:-}" ]; then
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6800567Z       # If PKEXEC_UID is set, use the user associated with it
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6801169Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(id -n -u "${PKEXEC_UID}")
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6801864Z     else
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6802567Z       # Try to find another user who is not root and has /usr/bin/bash as their shell
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6803544Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(grep '/usr/bin/bash' /etc/passwd | grep -v '^root' | cut -d: -f1 | head -n 1)
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6804210Z
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6804585Z       # If no such user is found, use the current user running the script
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6805236Z       if [ -z "${INSTALLER_USER:-}" ]; then
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6805817Z         INSTALLER_USER=$(id -un)
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6806349Z       fi
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6806621Z     fi
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6806833Z
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6807053Z     # Get the home directory of the selected user
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6807484Z     HOME_DIR=$(grep "^$INSTALLER_USER:" /etc/passwd | cut -d: -f6)
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6807865Z
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6808200Z     # Check if the home directory exists, otherwise create it
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6808780Z     if [ ! -d "$HOME_DIR" ]; then
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6809792Z       echo "The home directory $HOME_DIR does not exist. Creating it." >&2
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6810458Z       mkdir -p "$HOME_DIR"
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6810917Z       chown "$INSTALLER_USER" "$HOME_DIR"
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6811405Z     else
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6811847Z       echo "Home directory $HOME_DIR already exists." >&2
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6812386Z     fi
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6812659Z
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6812977Z     # Return the user and home directory for further use
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6813524Z     echo "$INSTALLER_USER"
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6813948Z }
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6814216Z
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6814468Z get_default_browser() {
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6815169Z     # Read the default browser from the anaconda runtime configuration file but wait till it's available
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6816302Z     # FIXME: Related to: https://issues.redhat.com/browse/INSTALLER-4223
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6819866Z     timeout=60
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6820296Z     elapsed=0
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6820606Z
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6820934Z     while [ ! -f /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf ]; do
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6821519Z         if [ "$elapsed" -ge "$timeout" ]; then
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6822131Z             echo "Timed out waiting for anaconda.conf to be available." >&2
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6822718Z             exit 1
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6823067Z         fi
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6823436Z         sleep 1
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6823865Z         elapsed=$((elapsed + 1))
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6824319Z     done
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6824607Z
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6825192Z     # FIXME: Should be provided as an argument to this script instead of grepping the config file
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6826128Z     DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(grep '^webui_web_engine =' /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf | cut -d'=' -f2)
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6827044Z     if [ -n "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" ]; then
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6827652Z         echo "Default browser set in anaconda.conf: $DEFAULT_BROWSER" >&2
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6828475Z         DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" | sed 's/^[[:space:]]*//;s/[[:space:]]*$//')
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6829224Z         echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER"
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6829873Z     else
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6830383Z         echo "No default browser set in anaconda.conf, using firefox." >&2
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6830996Z         echo "firefox"
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6831407Z     fi
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6831759Z }
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6832027Z
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6832283Z prepare_firefox_cmd() {
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6832712Z     INSTALLER_USER=$1
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6833019Z
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6833441Z     # prepare empty firefox profile dir with theme based on the passed profile id
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6834154Z     FIREFOX_THEME_DIR="/usr/share/anaconda/firefox-theme"
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6834817Z     FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH="${XDG_RUNTIME_DIR}/anaconda/firefox-profile"
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6835325Z
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6835642Z     # make sure the profile directory exists and is empty
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6836190Z     if [ -d ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH} ]
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6836825Z     then
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6837370Z         echo "Cleaning up existing Anaconda Firefox profile directory." >&2
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6838419Z         rm -rf ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH}
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6838948Z     fi
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6839700Z     pkexec --user "${INSTALLER_USER}" mkdir -p ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH}
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6840285Z
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6840655Z     # populate the profile directory with our custom Firefox theme
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6841359Z     # - theme id is passed as the second argument of this script
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6841982Z     THEME_PATH="${FIREFOX_THEME_DIR}/${THEME_ID}"
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6842400Z
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6842832Z     pkexec --user "${INSTALLER_USER}" cp -a "${THEME_PATH}/." ${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH}
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6843411Z
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6843678Z     # set environment for Firefox
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6844414Z     BROWSER_ENV=(MOZ_APP_TITLE="" MOZ_APP_REMOTINGNAME="liveinst" MOZ_GTK_TITLEBAR_DECORATION=client)
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6845084Z
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6845653Z     BROWSER=(/usr/bin/env "${BROWSER_ENV[@]}" /usr/bin/firefox --new-instance --profile "${FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH}")
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6846343Z
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6846607Z     declare -p BROWSER
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6847021Z }
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6847292Z
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6847438Z
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6847704Z if [[ "$WEBUI_REMOTE" == "1" ]]
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6848144Z then
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6848521Z     /bin/sleep infinity &
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6848951Z     BLOCK_ON_PID=$!
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6849338Z else
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6850069Z     USER_INFO=$(setup_user_environment)
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6850630Z     INSTALLER_USER=$(echo "$USER_INFO" | cut -d' ' -f1)
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6851204Z     HOME_DIR=$(echo "$USER_INFO" | cut -d' ' -f2)
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6851623Z
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6851945Z     # if we have netcat, use it for waiting until ws is up
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6852499Z     if type nc >/dev/null 2>&1; then
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6852982Z         for _ in `seq 10`; do
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6853475Z             nc -z "$WEBUI_ADDRESS" 80 && break
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6853968Z             sleep 0.5;
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6854372Z         done
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6854746Z     else
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6855132Z         # otherwise, just wait a bit
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6855602Z         sleep 3
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6856012Z     fi
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6856307Z
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6857062Z     readarray -t user_environment < <(pkexec --user "${INSTALLER_USER}" env XDG_RUNTIME_DIR="${XDG_RUNTIME_DIR}" systemctl --user show-environment)
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6857963Z
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6858258Z     DEFAULT_BROWSER_CMD=$(get_default_browser)
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6858879Z     echo "Using default browser: ${DEFAULT_BROWSER_CMD}" >&2
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6859922Z     if [[ "${DEFAULT_BROWSER_CMD}" == "firefox" ]]; then
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6860511Z         eval "$(prepare_firefox_cmd "$INSTALLER_USER")"
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6866315Z         echo "Using Firefox browser command: ${BROWSER[@]}" >&2
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6867022Z     else
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6867436Z         BROWSER=(${DEFAULT_BROWSER_CMD})
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6868011Z         echo "Using default browser command: ${BROWSER[@]}" >&2
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6868557Z     fi
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6868893Z

2026-10-24T04:36:09.6879820Z # Check if gettext exists
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6880293Z if ! type gettext > /dev/null 2>&1; then
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6880890Z   # If not, create a dummy function that returns the input verbatim
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6881489Z   gettext() { printf '%s' "$1"; }
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6881951Z fi
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6882222Z
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6882577Z # returns OK if $1 contains literal string $2 (and isn't empty)
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6883133Z strstr() {
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6883512Z     [ "${1##*"$2"*}" != "$1" ]
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6883942Z }
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6884256Z
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6884522Z # Run some action. Log its output.
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6884969Z action() {
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6885343Z     local STRING rc
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6885646Z
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6885883Z     STRING=$1
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6886291Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6886756Z     shift
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6887115Z     if "$@"; then
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6887524Z         success "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6888011Z     else
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6888419Z         failure "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6889250Z     fi
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6889850Z     rc=$?
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6890230Z     printf '\n'
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6890629Z     return $rc
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6891010Z }
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6891281Z
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6891638Z # verbose ->> very (very!) old bootup look (prior to RHL-6.0?)
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6892232Z # color ->> default bootup look
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6892804Z # other ->> default bootup look without ANSI colors or positioning
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6893396Z BOOTUP=color
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6893802Z # Column to start "[  OK  ]" label in:
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6894269Z RES_COL=60
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6894683Z # terminal sequence to move to that column:
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6895205Z MOVE_TO_COL="printf \\033[${RES_COL}G"
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6895793Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'success' (bright green):
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6896420Z SETCOLOR_SUCCESS="printf \\033[1;32m"
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6897003Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'failure' (bright red):
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6897606Z SETCOLOR_FAILURE="printf \\033[1;31m"
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6898237Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'warning' (bright yellow):
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6899008Z SETCOLOR_WARNING="printf \\033[1;33m"
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6899653Z # Terminal sequence to reset to the default color:
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6900208Z SETCOLOR_NORMAL="printf \\033[0;39m"
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6900583Z
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6900863Z # NOTE: /dev/ttyS* is serial console.
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6901440Z # 'not a tty' is reported when executed under systemd service units
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6902087Z # where /dev/null is associated with standard input
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6902527Z
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6902782Z test_tty=$(LC_ALL=C tty)
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6903225Z case "$test_tty" in
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6903637Z     /dev/ttyS* | 'not a tty')
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6904089Z         BOOTUP=serial
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6904496Z         MOVE_TO_COL=
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6904903Z         SETCOLOR_SUCCESS=
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6905332Z         SETCOLOR_FAILURE=
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6905768Z         SETCOLOR_WARNING=
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6906230Z         SETCOLOR_NORMAL=
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6906672Z         ;;
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6907071Z esac
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6907435Z
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6907770Z # Log that something succeeded
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6908252Z success() {
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6908822Z     [ "$BOOTUP" != "verbose" ] && echo_success
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6909321Z     return 0
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6909817Z }
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6910091Z
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6910359Z # Log that something failed
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6910827Z failure() {
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6911204Z     local rc=$?
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6911612Z     [ "$BOOTUP" != "verbose" ] && echo_failure
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6912074Z     [ -x /bin/plymouth ] && /bin/plymouth --details
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6912439Z     return $rc
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6912717Z }
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6912919Z
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6913107Z echo_success() {
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6913420Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $MOVE_TO_COL
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6913870Z     printf '%s' "["
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6918053Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_SUCCESS
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6918751Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "  OK  ")"
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6919263Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_NORMAL
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6919980Z     printf '%s' "]"
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6920393Z     printf '\r'
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6920847Z     return 0
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6921226Z }
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6921499Z
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6921744Z echo_failure() {
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6922158Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $MOVE_TO_COL
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6922652Z     printf '%s' "["
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6923100Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_FAILURE
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6923633Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "FAILED")"
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6924155Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_NORMAL
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6924649Z     printf '%s' "]"
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6925049Z     printf '\r'
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6925419Z     return 1
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6925785Z }
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6926068Z
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6926323Z fscheck() {
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6926709Z     local fstype="$1"
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6927211Z     local fs="$2"
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6927650Z     local d="${3:-$2}"
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6928093Z     umount "$fs" >/dev/null 2>&1 || :
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6928571Z     case "$fstype" in
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6928983Z         ext[432])
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6929389Z             printf '\n'
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6930013Z             flock "$d" e2fsck -yfv "$fs" || :
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6930505Z             ;;
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6930897Z         vfat|msdos)
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6931299Z             printf '\n'
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6931743Z             flock "$d" fsck.fat -avVw "$fs"
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6932226Z             ;;
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6932603Z         btrfs)
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6933040Z             printf '# btrfs check -p --repair --force'
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6933623Z             flock "$d" btrfs check -p --repair --force "$fs"
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6934141Z             ;;
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6934506Z         xfs)
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6934901Z             printf '\n# xfs_repair -v'
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6935567Z             flock "$d" xfs_repair -v "$fs"
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6936030Z             ;;
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6936395Z         f2fs)
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6936784Z             printf '# fsck.f2fs -afy'
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6937273Z             flock "$d" fsck.f2fs -afy "$fs"
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6937734Z             ;;
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6938102Z         *)
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6938588Z             printf "\n\tERROR:   Filesystem checking for '%s' is not\n \
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6939529Z                 available.\n" "$fstype"
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6940094Z     esac
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6940468Z }

2026-10-24T04:36:09.6988775Z mountPersistentHome() {
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6989214Z   # support label/uuid
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6989877Z   if [ "${homedev##LABEL=}" != "$homedev" ] ||
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6990409Z     [ "${homedev##UUID=}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6991337Z     homedev=$(blkid -o device -t "$homedev")
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6991839Z   fi
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6992117Z
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6992460Z   # if we're given a file rather than a blockdev, loop mount it
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6993083Z   if [ "${homedev##mtd}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6993720Z     # mtd devs don't have a block device but get magic-mounted with -t jffs2
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6994352Z     mountopts="-t jffs2"
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6994804Z   elif [ ! -b "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6995359Z     if [ "${homedev##/run/initramfs/live}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6996090Z       action "Remounting live store r/w" mount -o remount,rw /run/initramfs/live
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6996728Z     fi
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6997136Z     homedev=$(losetup -f --show "$homedev")
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6997632Z   fi
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6998091Z   homefs=$(blkid -s TYPE -o value "$homedev" 2>/dev/null)
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6998547Z
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6998939Z   # if it's encrypted, we need to unlock it
2026-10-24T04:36:09.6999688Z   if [ "$homefs" = "crypto_LUKS" ]; then
2026-10-24T04:36:09.7000239Z     echo
2026-10-24T04:36:09.7000654Z     echo "Setting up encrypted /home device"
2026-10-24T04:36:09.7001516Z     plymouth ask-for-password --command="cryptsetup luksOpen $homedev EncHome"
2026-10-24T04:36:09.7002201Z     homedev=/dev/mapper/EncHome
2026-10-24T04:36:09.7002645Z   fi
2026-10-24T04:36:09.7003146Z   if [ "$homedev" ] && [ "${cmdline##* rd.skipfsck[= ]}" = "$cmdline" ]; then
